<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01FgmRyCwtL6bhq113DsZUWF`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	name: review-director-session-start
3	description: Review Director session 启动初始化流程——圆桌多轮审稿协议。Review Director 角色 session 启动时自动使用。
4	priority: 9
5	scope: global
6	tags:
7	- workflow
8	- review
9	- session
10	version: 3
11	roles:
12	- review_director
13	---
14	
15	# Review Director Session 启动流程（圆桌会议模式）
16	
17	## 初始化
18	
19	1. `director_get_project_overview(project)` — 了解研究现状（实验状态、最新报告）
20	2. 读取论文文件状态：检查 `projects/{project}/paper/` 或 `main.tex` 是否存在
21	3. 检查是否有来自 Research Director 的论文提交（`[论文提交]` 消息）
22	4. 向用户简要汇报：
23	   - **投稿目标**：venue 和 deadline
24	   - **论文状态**：当前草稿位置和版本
25	   - **资料完整性**：提交的材料是否齐全
26	   - **下一步**：即将开始的圆桌审稿流程
27	
28	## 评审流程（收到论文提交后执行）
29	
30	### Step 0 — 资料检查 + Venue 调研（必须先做，不可跳过）
31	
32	1. 检查提交资料完整性：论文文件是否存在、是否可读、补充材料是否齐全
33	2. 确认目标投稿 venue（从提交消息中获取）
34	3. 用 `web_search` 搜索该 venue 当前轮次的 CFP（Call for Papers），关键词示例：
35	   - `"&lt;venue name> 2026 call for papers" site:aclweb.org`
36	   - `"&lt;venue name> 2026 submission deadline"`
37	   - `"&lt;venue name> 2026 author guidelines"`
38	4. **验证 deadline 未过期**：今天日期与截止日期对比，若已过期立即通知用户，不继续
39	5. 提取当前版本的格式要求：
40	   - 页数限制（正文 / 附录 / 参考文献）
41	   - 模板（ACL / IEEE / NeurIPS / ICML / ...）
42	   - 匿名规则（double-blind / single-blind / open）
43	   - 附录规则（是否允许、是否参与审稿）
44	   - 引用格式（natbib / biblatex / 特定 .bst）
45	6. 将以上信息作为上下文，随任务一起传递给 Reviewer
46	
47	⚠️ 禁止使用训练知识中的 venue 要求——格式标准每年都会更新，必须实时搜索确认。
48	
49	### Step 0.5 — 决定 Reviewer 编排
50	
51	根据 venue 类型和论文特点，决定：
52	- 需要几个 Reviewer（1-5 人）
53	- 每个 Reviewer 的审查侧重
54	
55	参考：
56	- **顶会长文**（NeurIPS/ICML/ACL）：3 人（方法/实验 + novelty/related work + 格式/ethics）
57	- **一般会议**（EMNLP/NAACL）：2 人（方法严谨性 + 新颖性/完整性）
58	- **Workshop / 短文**：1 人（全面审查）
59	
60	向 Group C（即项目配置中的 `feishu.review_chat_id` 审稿群）汇报编排决定。
61	
62	### Step 0.6 — 外部审稿线（与 Step 1 并行）
63	
64	检查项目配置中 `external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled` 是否为 `true`：
65	- 如果启用：调用 `request_external_review(project, paper_pdf_path, venue)` 向用户群发送协助提交卡片
66	- 这一步与 Step 1（内部 Reviewer 分配）**并行执行**，不阻塞内部审稿流程
67	- 外部结果到达后（用户在群里粘贴），纳入 Step 4 最终报告
68	- 如果到 Step 4 时外部结果仍未返回，调用 `get_external_review_status(project)` 确认状态，在报告中标注
69	
70	### Step 1 — Round N：独立审稿
71	
72	携带 Step 0 搜到的 venue 要求，启动所有 Reviewer：
73	
74	```
75	director_post_to_reviewer(reviewer_id="1", round_number=N, text="你负责 {focus}。...")
76	director_post_to_reviewer(reviewer_id="2", round_number=N, text="你负责 {focus}。...")
77	...（按需）
78	```
79	
80	消息中必须包含：
81	- venue 名称和截止日期
82	- 页数限制和模板名称
83	- 匿名规则
84	- 论文文件路径
85	- **该 Reviewer 的审查侧重**（在消息中明确指定）
86	
87	收集所有 Reviewer 的审稿结果。
88	
89	### Step 2 — 交叉分享（原文转发 + 逐条回应）
90	
91	将每位 Reviewer 的**完整审稿原文**转发给其他 Reviewer，要求逐条回应：
92	
93	```
94	director_post_to_reviewer(reviewer_id="2", round_number=N, text="""
95	以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见：
96	
97	{Reviewer 1 的原文}
98	
99	请逐条回应：[AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。
100	""")
101	```
102	
103	对每位 Reviewer 重复此步骤（每人看到其他所有 Reviewer 的原文）。
104	
105	收集所有交叉回应。
106	
107	### Step 3 — 收敛判断
108	
109	分析所有 Reviewer 的意见和交叉回应：
110	
111	- **关键问题已达成共识**（`[AGREE]` 占多数，无未解决的 `[REBUT]` 矛盾）→ 进入 Step 4
112	- **存在重大分歧**（关键 issue 上有 `[REBUT]` 且双方各有道理）→ 回到 Step 1 开始下一轮，附带聚焦问题
113	- **最多 3 轮**：如果第 3 轮仍有分歧，在报告中如实记录各方观点
114	
115	### Step 4 — 汇总报告 & 投递
116	
117	合并所有轮次的发现，形成统一审稿报告：
118	
119	1. 报告结构：
120	   - **Summary**：论文概述（2-3 句）
121	   - **Strengths**：优点（逐条列出）
122	   - **Weaknesses**：缺点（逐条列出，这是重点，必须严格）
123	   - **Reviewer Consensus**：所有 Reviewer 达成共识的关键问题（标注 `[AGREE]`）
124	   - **Reviewer Divergence**：分歧点及各方理由（标注 `[REBUT]`，如有）
125	   - **Questions**：需要作者澄清的问题
126	   - **Missing References**：遗漏的重要相关工作
127	   - **External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)**：如有外部审稿结果，单独列出其 7 维评分、关键发现、以及与内部审稿的差异；如未返回则标注状态
128	   - **Overall Assessment**：总评 + 评分（strong reject / weak reject / borderline / weak accept / strong accept）+ 置信度
129	   - **Detailed Comments**：逐节详细意见
130	2. **同步 Issue Tracker**（投递前必做）：
131	   - 用 `read_local_file("projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml")` 读当前 tracker（不存在视为空）
132	   - 对本轮所有 Weakness/Must-Fix/Should-Fix:
133	     - 与已有 issue 匹配（同 category + 相似 summary）→ 在 `mentions` 追加本轮记录,更新 `last_seen_round`
134	     - 新问题 → 创建条目（id=`R{reviewer}-W{n}`,status=`open`,first_raised_round=本轮）
135	   - 对上轮 `open`/`in_progress` 但本轮**未被任何 Reviewer 提及**的:
136	     - 在 `mentions` 追加 `note: "本轮未提及,可能已修复,待 Research Director 确认"`
137	   - 调 `update_review_issues(project, issues_yaml=完整 YAML)` 写回
138	3. 调用 `submit_review_report_to_research_director` 投递报告 → Research Director
139	4. 向用户（Group C）汇报审核完成
140	
141	此流程只在 session 启动时执行一次。
142	
````

</details>

      "content": "---\nname: review-director-session-start\ndescription: Review Director session 启动初始化流程——圆桌多轮审稿协议。Review Director 角色 session 启动时自动使用。\npriority: 9\nscope: global\ntags:\n- workflow\n- review\n- session\nversion: 3\nroles:\n- review_director\n---\n\n# Review Director Session 启动流程（圆桌会议模式）\n\n## 初始化\n\n1. `director_get_project_overview(project)` — 了解研究现状（实验状态、最新报告）\n2. 读取论文文件状态：检查 `projects/{project}/paper/` 或 `main.tex` 是否存在\n3. 检查是否有来自 Research Director 的论文提交（`[论文提交]` 消息）\n4. 向用户简要汇报：\n   - **投稿目标**：venue 和 deadline\n   - **论文状态**：当前草稿位置和版本\n   - **资料完整性**：提交的材料是否齐全\n   - **下一步**：即将开始的圆桌审稿流程\n\n## 评审流程（收到论文提交后执行）\n\n### Step 0 — 资料检查 + Venue 调研（必须先做，不可跳过）\n\n1. 检查提交资料完整性：论文文件是否存在、是否可读、补充材料是否齐全\n2. 确认目标投稿 venue（从提交消息中获取）\n3. 用 `web_search` 搜索该 venue 当前轮次的 CFP（Call for Papers），关键词示例：\n   - `\"&lt;venue name> 2026 call for papers\" site:aclweb.org`\n   - `\"&lt;venue name> 2026 submission deadline\"`\n   - `\"&lt;venue name> 2026 author guidelines\"`\n4. **验证 deadline 未过期**：今天日期与截止日期对比，若已过期立即通知用户，不继续\n5. 提取当前版本的格式要求：\n   - 页数限制（正文 / 附录 / 参考文献）\n   - 模板（ACL / IEEE / NeurIPS / ICML / ...）\n   - 匿名规则（double-blind / single-blind / open）\n   - 附录规则（是否允许、是否参与审稿）\n   - 引用格式（natbib / biblatex / 特定 .bst）\n6. 将以上信息作为上下文，随任务一起传递给 Reviewer\n\n⚠️ 禁止使用训练知识中的 venue 要求——格式标准每年都会更新，必须实时搜索确认。\n\n### Step 0.5 — 决定 Reviewer 编排\n\n根据 venue 类型和论文特点，决定：\n- 需要几个 Reviewer（1-5 人）\n- 每个 Reviewer 的审查侧重\n\n参考：\n- **顶会长文**（NeurIPS/ICML/ACL）：3 人（方法/实验 + novelty/related work + 格式/ethics）\n- **一般会议**（EMNLP/NAACL）：2 人（方法严谨性 + 新颖性/完整性）\n- **Workshop / 短文**：1 人（全面审查）\n\n向 Group C（即项目配置中的 `feishu.review_chat_id` 审稿群）汇报编排决定。\n\n### Step 0.6 — 外部审稿线（与 Step 1 并行）\n\n检查项目配置中 `external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled` 是否为 `true`：\n- 如果启用：调用 `request_external_review(project, paper_pdf_path, venue)` 向用户群发送协助提交卡片\n- 这一步与 Step 1（内部 Reviewer 分配）**并行执行**，不阻塞内部审稿流程\n- 外部结果到达后（用户在群里粘贴），纳入 Step 4 最终报告\n- 如果到 Step 4 时外部结果仍未返回，调用 `get_external_review_status(project)` 确认状态，在报告中标注\n\n### Step 1 — Round N：独立审稿\n\n携带 Step 0 搜到的 venue 要求，启动所有 Reviewer：\n\n```\ndirector_post_to_reviewer(reviewer_id=\"1\", round_number=N, text=\"你负责 {focus}。...\")\ndirector_post_to_reviewer(reviewer_id=\"2\", round_number=N, text=\"你负责 {focus}。...\")\n...（按需）\n```\n\n消息中必须包含：\n- venue 名称和截止日期\n- 页数限制和模板名称\n- 匿名规则\n- 论文文件路径\n- **该 Reviewer 的审查侧重**（在消息中明确指定）\n\n收集所有 Reviewer 的审稿结果。\n\n### Step 2 — 交叉分享（原文转发 + 逐条回应）\n\n将每位 Reviewer 的**完整审稿原文**转发给其他 Reviewer，要求逐条回应：\n\n```\ndirector_post_to_reviewer(reviewer_id=\"2\", round_number=N, text=\"\"\"\n以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见：\n\n{Reviewer 1 的原文}\n\n请逐条回应：[AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。\n\"\"\")\n```\n\n对每位 Reviewer 重复此步骤（每人看到其他所有 Reviewer 的原文）。\n\n收集所有交叉回应。\n\n### Step 3 — 收敛判断\n\n分析所有 Reviewer 的意见和交叉回应：\n\n- **关键问题已达成共识**（`[AGREE]` 占多数，无未解决的 `[REBUT]` 矛盾）→ 进入 Step 4\n- **存在重大分歧**（关键 issue 上有 `[REBUT]` 且双方各有道理）→ 回到 Step 1 开始下一轮，附带聚焦问题\n- **最多 3 轮**：如果第 3 轮仍有分歧，在报告中如实记录各方观点\n\n### Step 4 — 汇总报告 & 投递\n\n合并所有轮次的发现，形成统一审稿报告：\n\n1. 报告结构：\n   - **Summary**：论文概述（2-3 句）\n   - **Strengths**：优点（逐条列出）\n   - **Weaknesses**：缺点（逐条列出，这是重点，必须严格）\n   - **Reviewer Consensus**：所有 Reviewer 达成共识的关键问题（标注 `[AGREE]`）\n   - **Reviewer Divergence**：分歧点及各方理由（标注 `[REBUT]`，如有）\n   - **Detailed Comments**：逐节详细意见\n2. **同步 Issue Tracker**（投递前必做）：\n   - 用 `read_local_file(\"projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml\")` 读当前 tracker（不存在视为空）\n   - 对本轮所有 Weakness/Must-Fix/Should-Fix:\n     - {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_2/projects/tax_error_inject_agent/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory — tax_error_inject_agent\\n\\n## 关键发现\\n\\n- **v4 正式判定: Gate 3a FAIL, Gate 3b FAIL**: TaxEI 61.7% = vanilla 61.7%, random 66.0% 反超。Taxonomy 标签在 clean-task eval 上无额外价值。所有 McNemar 检验无显著性（n=47, discordant=10）。\\n- **Recovery eval 同样 FAIL**: TaxEI 59.6% ≤ Vanilla 61.7%，head-to-head gained 4 lost 5 net -1。Taxonomy 标签在 recovery 场景也无系统性优势。\\n- **Detection rate 全 0%**: 所有模型（vanilla/taxei/random）detection_rate=0。当前 eval 框架只检查最终任务成功，没有机制捕捉\\\"模型是否意识到错误发生\\\"。Detection 指标在当前框架下无意义——这是 eval 设计局限，不是模型能力结论。\\n- **[2026-05-15] Per-operator delta 修正 (D021)**: Fig 2 旧数据有 3/4 错误。修正后：C1=-8.3pp (was -5.6), C4=+9.1pp (was +6.1), P1=0.0pp (was -2.7), P2=-8.3pp (was -8.4)。Vanilla per-operator: C1=75.0%(9/12), C4=54.5%(6/11), P1=58.3%(7/12), P2=58.3%(7/12)。TaxEI per-operator: C1=66.7%(8/12), C4=63.6%(7/11), P1=58.3%(7/12), P2=50.0%(6/12)。核心 narrative 不变（C4 唯一正向，P2 最大负向），但 P1 实际为零差异。\\n- **C4 observation_masking 是 TaxEI 唯一正向 operator (+9.1pp)**: 暗示 taxonomy 标签可能只在信息缺失类错误上有微弱价值，但在 step 重排/删除类错误上反而有害。这个 pattern 值得在 negative paper 中讨论。\\n- **TaxEI 训练方差 3.3× vanilla** (SD=10.5pp vs 3.2pp): 注入导致训练极不稳定。这本身就是 negative finding——即使平均效果为零，高方差也意味着不可靠。\\n- **D012 \\\"系统性损害\\\" 结论 premature**: 方法差异与 seed 高度交互（seed=42 注入≈vanilla，seed=0 注入大幅下降）。不是稳定主效应，而是 noise-dominated。这对 paper framing 重要——不能说 \\\"systematically harms\\\"，应说 \\\"does not reliably improve and increases variance\\\"。\\n- **统计功效严重不足**: n=47 的 McNemar 需要 ~17pp 才有 power，而观察效应 (11pp 均值差) 与跨 seed 噪声 (10-15pp) 在同一量级。补充实验需要更多 seed 或更大 eval set。\\n- **TaxEI pick_two_obj 严重退化**: 28.6% vs vanilla/random 57.1%，完全抵消其他维度的微弱优势。\\n- **v3 vs v4 趋势不一致**: v3 vanilla>taxei>random, v4 random>taxei=vanilla。说明 v3 数据不平衡确实是混淆变量。\\n- **格式对齐修复有效**: v3-vanilla SFT 74.5% vs baseline 42.6%，+31.9pp。v1 的 14.9% 崩溃是训练/eval 格式不匹配。\\n- **GPT-5.2 ALFWorld 成功率 ~70%** (501/714)。\\n- **TaxEI v1 的\\\"保护效应\\\"是假象**: build_recovery_prompt() 恰好用了类似 eval 的格式。\\n- **[2026-05-15] permutation test bug 修复后所有比较不显著**: `multiseed_stats.py` 有浮点精度 bug（`mean(a)-mean(b)` vs `mean(a-b)` 差 ~1e-17，边界情况导致 p=0）。修复后 TaxEI vs Vanilla pooled p=0.017→**0.054 (NS)**，TaxEI vs Random p=0.051→0.170 (NS)。paper_framing 中 \\\"p=0.03\\\" 需更正。Narrative 应从 \\\"significant degradation\\\" 改为 \\\"directional but not statistically significant\\\"。修复方法: (1) `diff_obs` 改用 `np.mean(diffs)` 统一计算路径 (2) p 值改 `(count+1)/(n_perm+1)` 避免 p=0（Phipson & Smyth 2010）。\\n- **[2026-05-15] Random 3-seed 完整: 63.1% ± 6.8pp**: seed=42 66.0%, seed=0 55.3%, seed=123 68.1%。排序: Vanilla (65.3%) > Random (63.1%) > TaxEI (56.7%)，但所有 pairwise 均不显著。\\n- **[2026-05-15] Dose-response baseline 修正**: 所有 dose-response 实验 seed=42，应对比 vanilla seed=42 (61.7%) 非 3-seed 均值 (65.3%)。修正后: 10% injection → 0pp（非 -3.6pp），25% injection → -12.8pp（非 -16.4pp）。\\n- **[2026-05-15] TaxEI dose-response 呈阈值效应**: 0%→61.7%, 10%→61.7%(0pp), 25%→48.9%(-12.8pp), 50%→51.1%(-10.6pp)。25% 最差而非 50%。曲线在 ~15-20% 处存在 critical threshold——低于阈值无影响，高于阈值产生退化但不再持续恶化（plateau）。之前 R²=0.961 的线性拟合是两点假象。与 noise-ratio threshold 理论 (Rolnick 2017, Ye et al. ICLR 2025) 一致。\\n- **[2026-05-15] Random 10% 注入反而提升性能 (+6.4pp)**: Random 10% = 68.1% vs vanilla seed=42 61.7%。TaxEI 10% = 61.7% (0pp)。在低注入比例下，随机错误注入起到数据增强作用（增加轨迹多样性），而 taxonomy 标签限制了错误分布的多样性导致无效果。这是 TaxEI vs Random 的关键差异化证据——taxonomy 标签不仅无益，在低比例时还不如随机注入。\\n- **[2026-05-15] Training dynamics 悖论: 注入改善训练但恶化 eval**: 50% injection (taxei/random) final loss 0.26 vs vanilla 0.29, max acc 0.93 vs 0.91——训练指标全面优于 vanilla。但 eval: taxei 50%=51.1%, vanilla=61.7%。模型 overfit 到注入数据的分布（包括错误模式），train loss 下降反映的是对这些模式的记忆，而非对 clean task 的泛化。这对 paper 重要：可以论证 injection 不是简单的\\\"加噪声\\\"——模型确实学到了注入内容，只是学到的内容有害。\\n- **[2026-05-15] Training-eval mismatch 文献解释链**: Error injection → 训练分布偏移 (Longpre 2020) → 模型先学 clean pattern 再 memorize noise (Arpit 2017, Liu 2020) → SFT loss 优化的是 surface-level 拟合而非 downstream capability (Zhou 2023 LIMA) → 偏移的训练分布加剧 catastrophic forgetting，覆写预训练知识 (Luo 2023)。Injection 比例越高，链条每个环节都放大，导致 50% injection \\\"训练最好、eval 最差\\\"。\\n- **[2026-05-15] ⚠️ \\\"恒定 6.4pp taxonomy penalty\\\" 是离散化伪迹**: Per-task cross-analysis (art_20260515_d1414a) 确认：三个 dose 点（10%/25%/50%）中 TaxEI 失败但 Random 成功的 task 几乎完全不同。全部 3 个 dose 出现的 penalty task = **0 个**。2/3 出现 = 4 个（且分属不同 dose 对）。涉及的 penalty task 总计 9 个不同 task。47-task 粒度下分辨率 ≈ 2.13pp/task，Δ=3 tasks ≈ 6.4pp 是巧合，不是系统性惩罚。**Paper 中不能 claim \\\"constant additive taxonomy penalty\\\"**——应改为\\\"stochastic penalty that happens to average ~3 tasks per evaluation\\\"。\\n- **[2026-05-15] Per-task 3-seed 平均的 taxonomy gap 仍有参考价值**: pick_cool (-19pp) 和 pick_two (-14pp) 的 3-seed 平均 gap 可能反映 task-type 层面的趋势（需更多 seed 确认），但 dose-response 内的 per-task 翻转是随机的。两个层面的分析不矛盾：3-seed 看 task-type 趋势，dose-response 看 individual task 翻转。\\n- **[2026-05-15] D022 多 seed dose-response 完整结果**: 3-seed 平均 — TaxEI 25%=47.5%±1.2pp, TaxEI 50%=56.0%±12.5pp, Random 25%=56.0%±5.3pp, Random 50%=58.1%±11.7pp。vs Vanilla 65.3%±3.3pp。关键：(1) 25% 是最可靠分析窗口（TaxEI SD=1.2pp），taxonomy penalty 稳定 8.5pp；(2) 50% 注入方差爆炸（SD ~12pp），两方法趋同；(3) TaxEI 50% s0=70.2% 和 Random 50% s123=70.2% 互为镜像异常高值，说明高注入量训练极不稳定。\\n\\n## 文献调研关键洞察 (2026-05-14)\\n\\n- **PALADIN 数字已验证**: 32.76% → 89.68% RR (Gemma-3-12B) 准确。但 PALADIN 不是纯 SFT — 包含 inference-time retrie\\n\\n... 完整结果**: 3-seed 平均 — TaxEI 25%=47.5%±1.2pp, TaxEI 50%=56.0%±12.5pp, Random 25%=56.0%±5.3pp, Random 50%=58.1%±11.7pp。vs Vanilla 65.3%±3.3pp。关键：(1) 25% 是最可靠分析窗口（TaxEI SD=1.2pp），taxonomy penalty 稳定 8.5pp；(2) 50% 注入方差爆炸（SD ~12pp），两方法趋同；(3) TaxEI 50% s0=70.2% 和 Random 50% s123=70.2% 互为镜像异常高值，说明高注入量训练极不稳定。\\n\\n## 文献调研关键洞察 (2026-05-14)\\n\\n- **PALADIN 数字已验证**: 32.76% → 89.68% RR (Gemma-3-12B) 准确。但 PALADIN 不是纯 SFT — 包含 inference-time retrie\\n\\n... ### Tool result

_source line 30 · 2026-05-15T11:17:51.280Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01Gtf22QwbYWE3JASeQha1q4`</summary>

```text
1	\begin{abstract}
2	Taxonomy-guided error injection yields +57pp system-level improvement for tool-use LLM agents (PALADIN; combining taxonomy-guided injection, recovery pairing, and inference-time retrieval), but does this transfer to embodied planning? We test TaxEI---a four-operator error taxonomy---on ALFWorld with Qwen3-8B, comparing clean training (Vanilla), taxonomy-tagged injection (TaxEI), and generic-labeled injection (Random) across three seeds. TaxEI yields no statistically significant improvement ($-8.6$pp, $p{=}0.054$). However, this comparison is confounded: PALADIN pairs errors with recovery demonstrations, while our TaxEI injects standalone errors. The negative result may reflect missing recovery pairing rather than task structure. Beyond this negative result, we observe a dose-dependent dual regime: at moderate injection (25\%), performance degrades deterministically ($-17.8$pp, SD${=}$1.2pp, 3 seeds), while at high injection (50\%), extreme seed sensitivity emerges (SD${\approx}$12pp), with individual seeds ranging from 46.8\% to 70.2\%. We additionally observe a per-operator local/global split where local errors (+9.1pp) are learnable but global errors ($-8.3$pp) are harmful (exploratory, $N{=}11$--$12$). These patterns suggest that unpaired error injection may require discrete, observable, locally recoverable errors---conditions met in tool-use but not in embodied planning.
3	\end{abstract}
4

### Tool result

_source line 32 · 2026-05-15T11:17:51.805Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01DF6kGFaJ3mTDh6iDoNbuds`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	Training LLM agents on failure trajectories is an increasingly popular paradigm. PALADIN \cite{paladin2025} demonstrates that taxonomy-guided error injection---where training data includes systematically corrupted trajectories tagged with error types---yields +57pp system-level improvement for tool-use agents (combining taxonomy-guided injection, recovery pairing, and inference-time retrieval). Related approaches show gains across tool-use \cite{song2024eto,eef2025,wang2024learning_from_failure} and dialogue tasks \cite{agentHER2025}. A natural question arises: does this transfer to embodied planning, where agents must execute multi-step physical manipulation tasks?
5	
6	We test this on ALFWorld \cite{shridhar2021alfworld} with TaxEI, a four-operator error taxonomy for embodied planning, training Qwen3-8B agents under three conditions (Vanilla, TaxEI, Random) across three seeds. TaxEI yields no statistically significant improvement ($-8.6$pp, $p{=}0.054$) and directionally increases instability. Crucially, this comparison is confounded: PALADIN pairs each injected error with an explicit recovery demonstration, while our TaxEI injects standalone errors without paired recoveries. The negative result may therefore reflect missing recovery pairing rather than---or in addition to---differences in task structure.
7	
8	Beyond reporting this negative result, we make two contributions:
9	
10	\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
11	\item \textbf{Negative transfer with competing hypotheses.} Unpaired error injection provides no statistically significant benefit on ALFWorld. Two equally plausible explanations exist: (a)~embodied planning lacks the discrete, observable error structure that makes injection effective in tool-use, and (b)~the absence of recovery pairing renders injected errors unlearnable. Disentangling these requires recovery-paired injection experiments.
12	
13	\item \textbf{Dose-dependent dual regime.} At moderate injection (25\%), performance degrades deterministically ($-17.8$pp vs.\ vanilla, SD${=}$1.2pp, 3 seeds). At high injection (50\%), extreme seed sensitivity emerges (SD${\approx}$12pp), with individual seeds ranging from 46.8\% to 70.2\%---the latter exceeding all vanilla seeds. The dose-response is non-monotonic: 50\% mean (56.0\%) exceeds 25\% mean (47.5\%).
14	\end{enumerate}
15	
16	We additionally observe a per-operator local/global split where local errors are learnable but global errors are harmful (exploratory, $N{=}11$--$12$), and a training-evaluation paradox at $\geq$25\% injection. These exploratory patterns offer preliminary guidance for ALFWorld-like embodied tasks.
17	
```

```text
1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\subsection{Task Environment}
5	\label{sec:task_env}
6	
7	ALFWorld \cite{shridhar2021alfworld} is a text-based embodied environment where agents execute multi-step household tasks (e.g., \emph{heat a potato and place it in the fridge}). Each task requires a sequence of \texttt{think}/\texttt{act} steps in a ReAct-style loop \cite{yao2023react}. We use the standard 47-task unseen test split across six task types: \texttt{look\_at\_obj}, \texttt{pick\_and\_place}, \texttt{pick\_clean}, \texttt{pick\_cool}, \texttt{pick\_heat}, and \texttt{pick\_two\_obj}.
8	
9	\subsection{Error Taxonomy}
10	\label{sec:error_taxonomy}
11	
12	We define four error injection operators adapted for embodied planning, organized along two axes:
13	
14	\paragraph{Local operators} affect single steps without disrupting the overall plan structure:
15	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
16	\item \textbf{C4 (observation\_masking)}: Replaces environment observations with generic placeholders, removing perceptual grounding for one step.
17	\item \textbf{P1 (step\_order\_swap)}: Swaps two adjacent action steps, creating a locally incorrect but recoverable sequence.
18	\end{itemize}
19	
20	\paragraph{Global operators} disrupt the trajectory's causal chain:
21	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
22	\item \textbf{P2 (sub\_goal\_deletion)}: Removes a complete sub-goal (e.g., the ``clean'' step in a pick-clean-place task), breaking the plan's logical structure.
23	\item \textbf{C1 (context\_truncation)}: Truncates the context window, causing all subsequent decisions to rely on incomplete history.
24	\end{itemize}
25	
26	All operators achieve 100\% injection legality (valid action format) and 97.5\% recovery quality (trajectories remain parseable). Error-recovery pairs are \emph{not} explicitly paired in training data---errors are mixed as additional demonstrations.
27	
28	\subsection{Training Setup}
29	\label{sec:training_setup}
30	
31	We collect 501 expert trajectories from GPT-5.2 on ALFWorld training tasks, yielding 4{,}358 clean SFT demonstrations. Error injection produces 4{,}671 additional trajectories (round-robin across the four operators).
32	
33	\paragraph{Three conditions:}
34	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
35	\item \textbf{Vanilla}: 4{,}358 clean demonstrations only.
36	\item \textbf{TaxEI}: 4{,}358 clean + 4{,}671 taxonomy-tagged errors (error type labels in prompt) = 9{,}029 total.
37	\item \textbf{Random}: 4{,}358 clean + 4{,}671 generic-labeled errors (no taxonomy) = 9{,}029 total.
38	\end{itemize}
39	
40	All models fine-tune Qwen3-8B using LoRA (rank 16, $\alpha{=}32$, lr $1{\times}10^{-4}$, 3 epochs, bf16). Each condition is trained with three seeds (42, 0, 123).
41	
42	\paragraph{Dose-response ablation.} To control for data volume effects, we fix total training size at 4{,}358 and vary the error injection ratio (10\%, 25\%, 50\%). The 10\% condition uses seed=42 only; 25\% and 50\% are evaluated across three seeds (42, 0, 123).
43	
44	\paragraph{Recovery evaluation.} We inject errors at inference time using round-robin operator assignment across 47 test tasks and measure whether error-trained models recover better than vanilla.
```

</details>

```text
1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We evaluate three training conditions on ALFWorld's 47 unseen test tasks \cite{shridhar2021alfworld}: \textbf{Vanilla} (4{,}358 clean demonstrations), \textbf{TaxEI} (4{,}358 clean + 4{,}671 taxonomy-tagged error trajectories = 9{,}029), and \textbf{Random} (4{,}358 clean + 4{,}671 generic-labeled errors = 9{,}029). Training details are in \S\ref{sec:training_setup}. We report results across three seeds (42, 0, 123).
5	
6	\subsection{Main Results}
7	\label{sec:main_results}
8	
9	Table~\ref{tab:main_results} shows three-seed evaluation results. TaxEI degrades directionally ($-8.6$pp vs.\ Vanilla), but the difference is not statistically significant (permutation test, $p{=}0.054$, $N{=}10{,}000$). No TaxEI seed exceeds the Vanilla mean. Random injection shows minimal degradation ($-2.2$pp, $p{>}0.3$). The TaxEI--Random gap is driven by two of six task types (\texttt{pick\_cool}: $-19.0$pp; \texttt{pick\_two\_obj}: $-14.3$pp).
10	
11	\begin{table}[t]
12	\centering
13	\small
14	\begin{tabular}{lccccc}
15	\toprule
16	\textbf{Method} & \textbf{$N$} & \textbf{s42} & \textbf{s0} & \textbf{s123} & \textbf{Mean$\pm$SD} \\
17	\midrule
18	Vanilla & 4{,}358 & 61.7 & 68.1 & 66.0 & 65.3$\pm$3.3 \\
19	TaxEI   & 9{,}029 & 61.7 & 46.8 & 61.7 & 56.7$\pm$8.6 \\
20	Random  & 9{,}029 & 66.0 & 55.3 & 68.1 & 63.1$\pm$7.0 \\
21	\bottomrule
22	\end{tabular}
23	\caption{Three-seed clean evaluation on ALFWorld (47 tasks, accuracy \%). TaxEI vs.\ Vanilla: $p{=}0.054$; TaxEI vs.\ Random: $p{=}0.170$; Random vs.\ Vanilla: $p{>}0.3$ (pooled permutation, 10K). Note: TaxEI/Random use 9{,}029 samples ($2.1\times$ vanilla). The dose-response ablation (Table~\ref{tab:dose_response}) controls for data volume at a fixed 4{,}358 total.}
24	\label{tab:main_results}
25	\end{table}
26	
27	At full training scale, standard deviations follow Vanilla (3.3pp) $<$ Random (7.0pp) $<$ TaxEI (8.6pp), though not statistically confirmed at $n{=}3$. The dose-response ablation (\S\ref{sec:dose_response}) reveals this variance is dose-dependent: deterministic at 25\% (SD${=}$1.2pp) but chaotic at 50\% (SD${\approx}$12pp).
28	
29	\subsection{Dose-Response}
30	\label{sec:dose_response}
31	
32	To control for data volume, we fix total training size at 4{,}358 and vary the injection ratio (Table~\ref{tab:dose_response}, Figure~\ref{fig:dose_response}). Multi-seed results at 25\% and 50\% reveal a dual regime.
33	
34	\begin{table}[t]
35	\centering
36	\small
37	\begin{tabular}{lccccc}
38	\toprule
39	\textbf{Dose} & \textbf{s42} & \textbf{s0} & \textbf{s123} & \textbf{Mean$\pm$SD} & \textbf{$\Delta$} \\
40	\midrule
41	\multicolumn{6}{l}{\textit{TaxEI}} \\
42	10\%$^\dagger$ & 61.7 & --- & --- & 61.7 & $-$3.6 \\
43	25\%  & 48.9 & 46.8 & 46.8 & 47.5$\pm$1.2 & $-$17.8 \\
44	50\%  & 51.1 & 70.2 & 46.8 & 56.0$\pm$12.4 & $-$9.3 \\
45	\midrule
46	\multicolumn{6}{l}{\textit{Random}} \\
47	10\%$^\dagger$ & 68.1 & --- & --- & 68.1 & $+$2.8 \\
48	25\%  & 55.3 & 61.7 & 51.1 & 56.0$\pm$5.3 & $-$9.3 \\
49	50\%  & 57.4 & 46.8 & 70.2 & 58.1$\pm$11.7 & $-$7.2 \\
50	\bottomrule
51	\end{tabular}
52	\caption{Dose-response ablation (fixed 4{,}358 total). $\Delta$: mean change vs.\ vanilla (65.3$\pm$3.3). $^\dagger$Single seed. At 25\%, TaxEI degrades deterministically (SD${=}$1.2pp). At 50\%, variance explodes (SD${\approx}$12pp), with seeds spanning 46.8--70.2\%.}
53	\label{tab:dose_response}
54	\end{table}
55	
56	\begin{figure}[t]
57	\centering
58	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/paper/fig1_dose_response.pdf}
59	\caption{Dose-response (seed=42 only). 3-seed data in Table~\ref{tab:dose_response} reveals non-monotonic dose-response with regime bifurcation at 25\%$\to$50\%.}
60	\label{fig:dose_response}
61	\end{figure}
62	
63	At 25\%, TaxEI degrades deterministically: all three seeds converge to $\sim$47\% ($-17.8$pp, SD${=}$1.2pp). Random also degrades ($-9.3$pp, SD${=}$5.3pp). At 50\%, both methods exhibit extreme seed sensitivity (SD${\approx}$12pp), with seeds ranging from 46.8\% to 70.2\%---the latter exceeding all vanilla seeds. The dose-response is non-monotonic: 50\% TaxEI mean (56.0\%) exceeds 25\% mean (47.5\%). At 10\% (single seed), TaxEI matches Vanilla exactly; Random achieves 68.1\%, within vanilla seed range.
64	
65	\subsection{Recovery Evaluation}
66	\label{sec:recovery}
67	
68	We test whether error-trained models recover from injected errors at inference time (round-robin operator injection, 47 tasks, seed=42). TaxEI achieves 59.6\% recovery (vs.\ Vanilla 61.7\%, Random 63.8\%)---no statistically significant improvement. The uniform 0\% detection rate suggests taxonomy labels were not internalized during SFT---models learned trajectory patterns rather than error classification.
69	
70	\subsection{Per-Operator Local/Global Split}
71	\label{sec:operator_split}
72	
73	Figure~\ref{fig:operator_gradient} decomposes recovery by operator (seed=42, $N{=}11$--$12$). Local operators (C4 observation masking: 7/11 vs.\ 6/11, $+9.1$pp; P1 step swap: 7/12 vs.\ 7/12, $0.0$pp) are neutral-to-beneficial; global operators (C1 context truncation: 8/12 vs.\ 9/12, $-8.3$pp; P2 sub-goal deletion: 6/12 vs.\ 7/12, $-8.3$pp) are harmful. Local errors leave the causal structure intact; global errors propagate downstream. These results are exploratory ($N{=}11$--$12$).
74	
75	\begin{figure}[t]
76	\centering
77	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/paper/fig2_operator_gradient.pdf}
78	\caption{Per-operator recovery delta (TaxEI $-$ Vanilla, seed=42). $N{=}11$--$12$; $\pm1$ task $\approx$ $\pm$8--9pp. Exploratory.}
79	\label{fig:operator_gradient}
80	\end{figure}
81	
82	\subsection{Training-Evaluation Paradox}
83	\label{sec:paradox}
84	
85	At $\geq$25\% injection, models can achieve \emph{lower} training loss but \emph{worse} evaluation performance. For seed=42, TaxEI-50\% reaches final loss 0.259 (vs.\ Vanilla 0.288) yet evaluates at 51.1\% (vs.\ 61.7\%). This paradox does not hold at 10\%, where training loss (0.308) remains above Vanilla. Two explanations are plausible: memorization of noise \cite{arpit2017memorization,zhou2023lima} and distribution shift at high injection ratios. The extreme seed variance at 50\% suggests this paradox is seed-dependent: TaxEI-50\% seed=0 achieves 70.2\% despite similar training dynamics.
86	
```

At 50\\%, variance explodes (SD${\\approx}$12pp), with seeds spanning 46.8--70.2\\%.}\n\\label{tab:dose_response}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/paper/fig1_dose_response.pdf}\n\\caption{Dose-response (seed=42 only). 3-seed data in Table~\\ref{tab:dose_response} reveals non-monotonic dose-response with regime bifurcation at 25\\%$\\to$50\\%.}\n\\label{fig:dose_response}\n\\end{figure}\n\nAt 25\\%, TaxEI degrades deterministically: all three seeds converge to $\\sim$47\\% ($-17.8$pp, SD${=}$1.2pp). Random also degrades ($-9.3$pp, SD${=}$5.3pp). At 50\\%, both methods exhibit extreme seed sensitivity (SD${\\approx}$12pp), with seeds ranging from 46.8\\% to 70.2\\%---the latter exceeding all vanilla seeds. The dose-response is non-monotonic: 50\\% TaxEI mean (56.0\\%) exceeds 25\\% mean (47.5\\%). At 10\\% (single seed), TaxEI matches Vanilla exactly; Random achieves 68.1\\%, within vanilla seed range.\n\n\\subsection{Recovery Evaluation}\n\\label{sec:recovery}\n\nWe test whether error-trained models recover from injected errors at inference time (round-robin operator injection, 47 tasks, seed=42). TaxEI achieves 59.6\\% recovery (vs.\\ Vanilla 61.7\\%, Random 63.8\\%)---no statistically significant improvement. The uniform 0\\% detection rate suggests taxonomy labels were not internalized during SFT---models learned trajectory patterns rather than error classification.\n\n\\subsection{Per-Operator Local/Global Split}\n\\label{sec:operator_split}\n\nFigure~\\ref{fig:operator_gradient} decomposes recovery by operator (seed=42, $N{=}11$--$12$). Local operators (C4 observation masking: 7/11 vs.\\ 6/11, $+9.1$pp; P1 step swap: 7/12 vs.\\ 7/12, $0.0$pp) are neutral-to-beneficial; global operators (C1 context truncation: 8/12 vs.\\ 9/12, $-8.3$pp; P2 sub-goal deletion: 6/12 vs.\\ 7/12, $-8.3$pp) are harmful. Local errors leave the causal structure intact; global errors propagate downstream. These results are exploratory ($N{=}11$--$12$).\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/paper/fig2_operator_gradient.pdf}\n\\caption{Per-operator recovery delta (TaxEI $-$ Vanilla, seed=42). $N{=}11$--$12$; $\\pm1$ task $\\approx$ $\\pm$8--9pp. Exploratory.}\n\\label{fig:operator_gradient}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\subsection{Training-Evaluation Paradox}\n\\label{sec:paradox}\n\nAt $\\geq$25\\% injection, models can achieve \\emph{lower} training loss but \\emph{worse} evaluation performance. For seed=42, TaxEI-50\\% reaches final loss 0.259 (vs.\\ Vanilla 0.288) yet evaluates at 51.1\\% (vs.\\ 61.7\\%). This paradox does not hold at 10\\%, where training loss (0.308) remains above Vanilla. Two explanations are plausible: memorization of noise \\cite{arpit2017memorization,zhou2023lima} and distribution shift at high injection ratios. The extreme seed variance at 50\\% suggests this paradox is seed-dependent: TaxEI-50\\% seed=0 achieves 70.2\\% despite similar training dynamics.\n",
      "numLines": 86,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 86
    }

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01Cd5CVtHVppqrKAUHDHYPM3`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Structural hypothesis.}
5	We hypothesize that the transfer failure stems from structural differences between tool-use and embodied planning (Table~\ref{tab:structural} in Appendix). In tool-use, errors produce discrete signals (API error codes) mapping to specific recovery actions; taxonomy labels encode this mapping. In embodied planning, errors propagate silently---picking the wrong object produces no error message, and recovery requires replanning from an unknown state. Reflexion \cite{shinn2023reflexion} achieves $\sim$97\% on ALFWorld through inference-time failure reflection, suggesting that inference-time self-correction succeeds where training-time injection fails in embodied planning.
6	
7	\paragraph{Recovery-pairing confound.}
8	PALADIN pairs each error with an explicit recovery demonstration via retrieval augmentation, contributing an estimated $\sim$30pp of its +57pp system-level gain. Our TaxEI injects standalone errors without paired recoveries. We cannot distinguish between these two hypotheses with the current design: the negative result may stem from (a)~missing recovery pairing, or (b)~structural differences between tool-use and embodied planning, or both. Recovery-paired injection on ALFWorld is needed to disentangle these factors.
9	
10	\paragraph{Training lottery at high injection.}
11	At 50\% injection, individual seeds range from 46.8\% to 70.2\%---the latter exceeding all vanilla seeds. The 70.2\% anomaly occurs in both TaxEI (seed=0) and Random (seed=123), suggesting a generic high-dose phenomenon independent of taxonomy labeling. This cross-condition coincidence may reflect a seed-initialization $\times$ injection interaction warranting further investigation.
12	
13	\paragraph{Limitations.}
14	We evaluate on a single environment (ALFWorld, 47 tasks) with one base model. Three seeds provide limited statistical power; the 10\% dose-response uses a single seed (25\%/50\% confirmed across 3 seeds). TaxEI/Random use $2.1\times$ more training data than Vanilla; the fixed-size ablation controls for this. Per-operator analysis ($N{=}11$--$12$) is exploratory. ALFWorld uses a text-based interface where errors produce discrete feedback (``Nothing happens''), partially resembling tool-use error observability. Vision-based embodied tasks with continuous state spaces may exhibit different error injection dynamics. We compare only error injection variants; other augmentation strategies (e.g., paraphrasing, trajectory diversification) were not tested, leaving open whether the negative result generalizes to all off-distribution augmentation at this data scale.
15	
```

```text
1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Error injection for LLM agents.}
5	Several methods augment agent training with failure trajectories: PALADIN \cite{paladin2025} uses taxonomy-guided injection with retrieval-augmented recovery; ETO \cite{song2024eto} applies DPO on exploration failures; EEF \cite{eef2025} extracts positive signals from expert failures; Wang et al.\ \shortcite{wang2024learning_from_failure} and AgentHER \cite{agentHER2025} integrate contrastive or relabeled negatives. AgentDebug \cite{agentdebug2025} identifies five error dimensions including error cascading---a phenomenon our per-operator analysis independently corroborates. Their diagnostic taxonomy differs from our injection-oriented 4-operator design: AgentDebug classifies observed errors post-hoc, while our operators generate controlled perturbations. The overlap in cascading-error findings suggests this structural property is a robust moderator of error-injection effectiveness. Our work tests \emph{cross-domain transfer} of the injection paradigm using direct SFT mixing.
6	
7	\paragraph{Training dynamics and data mixing.}
8	McCoy et al.\ \shortcite{mccoy2020seeds} and Mosbach et al.\ \shortcite{mosbach2021stability} document seed sensitivity in fine-tuning; D'Amour et al.\ \shortcite{damour2022underspecification} frame this as underspecification. Our dose-dependent variance findings extend these observations to error-augmented agent training. Rolnick et al.\ \shortcite{rolnick2017robust} show robustness to massive label noise in vision---contrasting with our sharp degradation at 25\% injection in sequential decision-making. Ye et al.\ \shortcite{ye2025mixing_laws} and Bengio et al.\ \shortcite{bengio2009curriculum} establish that data composition has non-linear effects on SFT performance.
9	
10	\paragraph{ALFWorld methods.}
11	ReAct \cite{yao2023react} establishes think/act prompting. Reflexion \cite{shinn2023reflexion} achieves $\sim$97\% via multi-trial inference-time self-reflection---notably, inference-time reflection succeeds where our training-time injection fails. CoEx \cite{kim2025coex} reaches 93.3\% with co-evolving world models; AgentPRM \cite{agentprm2025} achieves 91.0\% with process reward models. Our 65.3\% vanilla baseline is consistent with single-trial SFT on an 8B model. No prior work has tested training-time error injection on ALFWorld.
12	
```

```text
1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We tested whether unpaired taxonomy-guided error injection---effective for tool-use agents---transfers to embodied planning. On ALFWorld, TaxEI provides no statistically significant improvement over clean training ($-8.6$pp, $p{=}0.054$). Two competing hypotheses explain this: embodied planning may lack the discrete, observable error structure required for injection to help, or the absence of recovery pairing (present in PALADIN) may render standalone errors unlearnable. We additionally identify a dose-dependent dual regime (3-seed confirmed at 25\%/50\%): deterministic degradation at moderate injection, chaotic seed-dependent outcomes at high injection. We also observe a per-operator local/global split (exploratory, $N{=}11$--$12$).
5	
6	Based on our results and the two competing hypotheses, we suggest that practitioners considering error injection for embodied tasks check four properties: (1)~are errors discrete and observable? (2)~can recovery be achieved locally? (3)~does the task provide immediate feedback? (4)~are errors paired with explicit recovery demonstrations? These criteria require validation in other environments.
7	
8	Future work should first test recovery-paired injection on ALFWorld to disentangle the task-structure hypothesis from recovery-pairing effects, then replicate on a second environment (e.g., WebShop) and investigate the seed-initialization $\times$ injection interaction observed at high doses.
9	
```

```text
1	\appendix
2	
3	\section{Structural Comparison}
4	\label{app:structural}
5	
6	\begin{table}[h]
7	\centering
8	\small
9	\begin{tabular}{p{2.2cm}p{2.3cm}p{2.3cm}}
10	\toprule
11	\textbf{Dimension} & \textbf{Tool-Use} \cite{paladin2025} & \textbf{Embodied} \\
12	\midrule
13	Error signal & Discrete (API error codes) & Diffuse (wrong object, wrong room) \\
14	Observability & Explicit (stack trace) & Silent (no error msg) \\
15	Recovery scope & Local (retry call) & Global (replan) \\
16	Feedback & Immediate & Delayed \\
17	Error locality & Single step & Cascading \\
18	\bottomrule
19	\end{tabular}
20	\caption{Structural comparison: tool-use vs.\ embodied planning. Error injection benefits from discrete, observable, locally recoverable errors.}
21	\label{tab:structural}
22	\end{table}
23	
24	\section{Training Dynamics}
25	\label{app:training_dynamics}
26	
27	Figure~\ref{fig:training_curves} shows training loss curves across conditions. At $\geq$25\% injection, models achieve lower final training loss than Vanilla (TaxEI-50\%: 0.259 vs.\ Vanilla: 0.288) despite worse evaluation performance (\S\ref{sec:paradox}). At 10\% injection, training loss (0.308) remains above Vanilla.
28	
29	\begin{figure}[t]
30	\centering
31	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/paper/fig_app_training_curves.pdf}
32	\caption{Left: training loss curves. Right: final loss vs.\ eval accuracy---lower loss corresponds to worse performance at $\geq$25\% injection.}
33	\label{fig:training_curves}
34	\end{figure}
35	
36	\section{Per-Task Cross-Analysis}
37	\label{app:cross_analysis}
38	
39	For seed=42, the $\sim$6.4pp aggregate gap between TaxEI and Random at each dose point corresponds to $\approx$3 tasks on a 47-task evaluation. This consistency does not hold across seeds: at 25\% the 3-seed mean gap is 8.5pp, at 50\% it is 2.1pp. Cross-dose analysis reveals this is a discretization coincidence: zero tasks appear as TaxEI-penalty in all three dose points (10\%, 25\%, 50\%). The specific tasks that flip between conditions rotate across doses---9 different tasks contribute to the penalty, with no single task appearing at all three levels.
40	
41	\section{Version Discrepancy}
42	\label{app:version}
43	
44	Our evaluation pipeline evolved from v3 to v4 during development. The v3 vanilla baseline (74.5\%, seed=42) was higher due to a format mismatch. The v3$\to$v4 transition involved three changes: (1)~prompt format alignment from ChatML to tool-format, (2)~sample count increase from 4{,}176 to 4{,}358 due to improved trajectory collection, and (3)~seed handling standardization. The 12.8pp drop (74.5\%$\to$61.7\%) is attributable primarily to the format alignment. All three conditions (Vanilla, TaxEI, Random) were trained and evaluated under the same v4 pipeline, ensuring fair internal comparisons.
45	
```

Based on the search results, I can provide you with information about EMNLP 2026's call for papers:

## EMNLP 2026 Call for Papers - Key Information

The ARR Submission Deadline for EMNLP 2026 is May 25, 2026.

### Regarding Negative Results

EMNLP 2026 has a goal of curating a diverse technical program—in addition to traditional research results, papers may contribute negative findings, survey an area, announce the creation of a new resource, argue a position, report novel linguistic insights derived using existing computational techniques, and reproduce (or fail to reproduce) previous results.

### General Submission Guidelines

EMNLP 2026 invites the submission of long and short papers featuring substantial, original, and unpublished research on empirical methods for Natural Language Processing. All deadlines are 11:59PM UTC-12:00 ("anywhere on Earth").

### Submission Process

EMNLP 2026 will use ACL Rolling Review (ARR) as a reviewing system, but final decisions will be made by the conference, with authors first submitting articles to ARR for review and then committing their reviewed articles to the conference.

The conference welcomes contributions including negative results as part of its commitment to a diverse technical program, making it well-suited for publishing insights from negative results in NLP research.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

Based on the search results, here's what I found about the author guidelines and page limits for the "Insights from Negative Results in NLP" workshop:

## 2025 Workshop (NAACL)

Short papers are limited to up to 4 pages plus references and appendices. The workshop also accepts 1-2 page non-archival abstracts for work published elsewhere.

## 2026 Workshop (EMNLP)

The workshop will take place in Budapest, Hungary, October 22-29, 2026, co-located with EMNLP. However, the specific author guidelines and page limits for the 2026 edition have not yet been published in the search results.

## Additional Note

There is also mention of an ICLR 2026 workshop proposal that accepts "tiny papers" with at most 2 pages of main text with the same structure and formatting instructions as full workshop submissions, though this appears to be a different workshop focused on large language models rather than the main Insights from Negative Results in NLP workshop.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

Based on the search results, I found information about the "Insights from Negative Results in NLP" workshop for 2026:

## Workshop Overview

The Workshop on Insights from Negative Results in NLP will be held in Budapest, Hungary, October 22-29, 2026 (co-located with EMNLP), with the exact day still to be announced.

## Workshop Purpose

The workshop invites both practical and theoretical unexpected or negative results that have important implications for future research, highlight methodological issues with existing approaches, and/or point out pervasive misunderstandings or bad practices.

## Submission Information

While I found information about the 2026 workshop location and co-location, the search results do not contain specific information about the submission deadline or call for papers details for the 2026 edition. However, you can confirm the workshop website at https://insights-workshop.github.io/2025/cfp/ for the submission link and more details (note: you may need to check the updated 2026 page when it becomes available).

For more current submission deadline information, I recommend visiting the official Insights workshop website directly.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

Based on the web search results, here are the key details about the Insights Workshop on Negative Results in NLP at NAACL 2025:

## Call for Papers and Submission Guidelines

Submission is electronic, using the Softconf START conference management system at https://softconf.com/naacl2025/Insights2025. ARR papers can be committed through the same page by specifying review type as "ARR review" and adding the openreview URL.

## Page Limits and Format

The workshop accepts short papers (up to 4 pages, excluding references, appendices, limitations and broader impacts sections), as well as 1-2 page non-archival abstract submissions for papers published elsewhere. Appendices should be included in the same file as the paper, and the number of appendix pages is not limited - but the paper has to be standalone work even if the appendices are not consulted at all.

## Workshop Details

The workshop will take place during NAACL 2025 (May 3-4, 2025) and will be hybrid, allowing for both in-person and virtual presentations.

## Contact Information

Any questions regarding submissions can be sent to insights-workshop-organizers@googlegroups.com.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

初始化\\n18\\t\\n19\\t1. `director_get_project_overview(project)` — 了解研究现状（实验状态、最新报告）\\n20\\t2. 读取论文文件状态：检查 `projects/{project}/paper/` 或 `main.tex` 是否存在\\n21\\t3. 检查是否有来自 Research Director 评审流程（收到论文提交后执行）\\n29\\t\\n30\\t### Step 0 — 资料检查 + Venue 调研（必须先做，不可跳过）\\n31\\t\\n32\\t1. 检查提交资料完整性：论文文件是否存在、是否可读、补充材料是否齐全\\n33\\t2. 确认目标投稿 venue（从提交消息中获取）\\n34\\t3. 用 `web_search` 搜索该 venue 当前轮次的 CFP（Call for Papers），关键词示例：\\n35\\t   - `\\\"&lt;venue name> 2026 call for papers\\\" site:aclweb.org`\\n36\\t   - `\\\"&lt;venue name> 2026 submission deadline\\\"`\\n37\\t   - `\\\"&lt;venue name> 2026 author guidelines\\\"`\\n38\\t4. **验证 deadline 未过期**：今天日期与截止日期对比，若已过期立即通知用户，不继续\\n39\\t5. 提取当前版本的格式要求：\\n40\\t   - 页数限制（正文 / 附录 / 参考文献）\\n41\\t   - 模板（ACL / IEEE / NeurIPS / ICML / ...）\\n42\\t   - 匿名规则（double-blind / single-blind / open）\\n43\\t   - 附录规则（是否允许、是否参与审稿）\\n44\\t   - 引用格式（natbib / biblatex / 特定 .bst）\\n45\\t6. 将以上信息作为上下文，随任务一起传递给 Reviewer\\n46\\t\\n47\\t⚠️ 禁止使用训练知识中的 venue 要求——格式标准每年都会更新，必须实时搜索确认。\\n48\\t\\n49\\t### Step 0.5 — 决定 Reviewer 编排\\n50\\t\\n51\\t根据 venue 类型和论文特点，决定：\\n52\\t- 需要几个 Reviewer（1-5 人）\\n53\\t- 每个 Reviewer 的审查侧重\\n54\\t\\n55\\t参考：\\n56\\t- **顶会长文**（NeurIPS/ICML/ACL）：3 人（方法/实验 + novelty/related work + 格式/ethics）\\n57\\t- **一般会议**（EMNLP/NAACL）：2 人（方法严谨性 + 新颖性/完整性）\\n58\\t- **Workshop / 短文**：1 人（全面审查）\\n59\\t\\n60\\t向 Group C（即项目配置中的 \"2026-05-15T07:24:13.032816+00:00\", \"role\": \"tool\", \"session\": \"feishu:group:oc_8acbea284219b548bfc4c50949ea43a8:director\", \"text\": \"called mcp__agent-ml-research__submit_review_report_to_research_director\", \"tool\": \"mcp__agent-ml-research__submit_review_report_to_research_director\", \"tool_args\": {\"report\": \"# 审稿报告 — Round 1\\n\\n**论文**: \\\"When Does Error Injection Help LLM Agents? Boundary Conditions from a Failed Transfer to Embodied Planning\\\"\\n**目标 Venue**: Insights from Negative Results in NLP @ EMNLP 2026\\n**审稿模式**: 2 Reviewer 圆桌，1 轮独立审稿 + 交叉回应，1 轮收敛\\n**收敛状态**: 关键问题高度共识，无需 Round 2\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Summary\\n\\n本文测试 taxonomy-guided error injection（TaxEI）从 tool-use（PALADIN）向 embodied planning（ALFWorld）的迁移。在 Qwen3-8B 上对比 Vanilla/TaxEI/Random 三条件（3 seeds），发现 TaxEI 无改善（−8.6pp, p=0.054）。论文提出三个 boundary conditions：variance gradient、dose-response threshold、per-operator local/global split。论文定位为 negative result paper，目标 venue 为 EMNLP 2026 Negative Results Workshop。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Strengths\\n\\n1. **选题精准，venue 匹配度高**：跨域迁移测试是自然的下一步，负面结果避免社区重复尝试。[R1, R2 共识]\\n2. **实验透明诚实**：Limitations 坦承几乎所有统计弱点，Version Discrepancy（Appendix D）透明披露，v3→v4 数据问题不隐瞒。[R1, R2 共识]\\n3. **超越简单报告失败**：dose-response、per-operator 分析和 structural comparison（Table 3）生成可检验假说。[R1]\\n4. **整体实验框架合理**：三条件 + 多 seed + dose-response 消融 + recovery eval 的设计逻辑完整。[R1]\\n5. **写作清晰流畅**：叙事有条理，实验细节充分（超参完整：LoRA rank 16, α=32, lr 1e-4, 3 epochs, bf16）。[R2]\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Weaknesses\\n\\n### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）\\n\\n**[MF-1] Recovery-pairing confound 使核心 \\\"迁移失败\\\" 结论不可靠** [R1-N3, R2-N2 | 双方最强共识]\\n- PALADIN = taxonomy tagging + recovery pairing + retrieval augmentation（~30pp 增益来自 retrieval）。TaxEI 仅复现了 taxonomy tagging。\\n- \\\"迁移失败\\\"无法区分：(a) error injection 在 embodied planning 本身无效 vs (b) 缺少 recovery pairing 导致失败。\\n- §5.2 承认了这点，但 Title/Abstract/Introduction/Conclusion 的 framing 暗示 (a)。\\n- **必须修改**：framing 明确限定为 \\\"unpaired error injection\\\" 的迁移测试；将 confound 从 Discussion 提升至 Introduction；两个竞争假说（task structure vs recovery pairing）并列为等权重解释。\\n\\n**[MF-2] Variance gradient 作为 boundary condition 无统计支撑** [R1-N2 | R2 强烈同意]\\n- 三组 SD=3.3/7.0/8.6pp，每组仅 n=3。所有检验均 NS（Levene's p=0.085, F-test p=0.252, bootstrap P=69%）。\\n- n=3 估 SD 的 95% CI 极宽（SD=3.3 → CI≈[1.9, 12.6]；SD=8.6 → CI≈[4.9, 33.0]），大幅重叠。\\n- 将统计上完全不支持的 pattern 列为论文第一个 boundary condition，违反 soundness 标准。\\n- **必须修改**：从 boundary conditions 列表中移除或降级为\\\"方向性观察\\\"，不在 Abstract 中以 claim 形式出现。\\n\\n**[MF-3] 数据量混淆与统计功效不足并存** [R1-N1 | R2 同意]\\n- 主表（Table 1）：TaxEI/Random 9,029 vs Vanilla 4,358（2.1×），有数据量混淆。\\n- 控制数据量的 dose-response 消融（Table 2）更公平，但仅用 seed=42（TaxEI 表现最好的 seed），可能系统性高估。\\n- 三个 boundary conditions 的证据基础全部建立在 underpowered 设计上。\\n- **必须修改**：(1) 将 Table 1 明确定位为\\\"保守估计——TaxEI 即使多出 2.1× 数据仍更差\\\"，把混淆转化为论证优势；(2) dose-response 补充 seeds 或明确标记为 preliminary；(3) 降低 claim 强度。\\n\\n**[MF-4] 主体文本超 4 页限制** [R2-N1, R1-W4 | 双方同意]\\n- PDF 确认 Conclusion 结束于 page 5 上半部，主体约 4.3–4.5 页。\\n- Insights workshop 短文 ≤ 4 pages excluding references，硬性格式违规。\\n- **必须修改**：大幅精简 Related Work（当前约 0.8 页），或将部分 Discussion 移至 Appendix。\\n\\n**[MF-5] PALADIN +57pp 归因不准确** [R2-N3, R1-W3 | 双方同意，R2 坚持 must-fix 级别]\\n- Abstract 第一句将 +57pp 归因于 error injection，但这是 PALADIN 完整系统增益（含 retrieval ~30pp）。\\n- 误导读者高估 error injection alone 的效果，扭曲论文出发点。\\n- **必须修改**：首次提及时拆分增益来源，区分 taxonomy injection 和 retrieval augmentation 的贡献。\\n\\n### Should-Fix\\n\\n**[SF-1] Per-operator local/global split 粒度过粗** [R1-W1 | R2 同意]\\n- N=11-12 per operator，单 seed，±1 task ≈ ±8-9pp。C4 的 +9.1pp 实际仅 +1 task。\\n- \\\"Binary split\\\" 措辞过强，可能是随机波动。\\n- 应报告 exact task count（如 \\\"7/11 vs 6/11\\\"），降级为 hypothesis-generating observation。\\n\\n**[SF-2] Training-evaluation paradox 缺替代假说** [R1-W2 | R2 部分反驳]\\n- 论文归因于 memorization，但 distribution shift 是更简单的竞争解释。\\n- R2 指出 10% 的非线性行为部分支持 memorization，但共识是应讨论两种解释。\\n- 在 §4.2 中增加 distribution shift 作为 competing explanation。\\n\\n**[SF-3] Boundary conditions 新颖性有限** [R2-W1 | R1 部分反驳]\\n- Variance gradient：\\\"加噪声增加不稳定性\\\"可预期，无新颖性。[共识]\\n- Dose-response threshold：R1 指出与 Rolnick 2017（vision 对 massive noise robust）的对比有新意。[分歧]\\n- Local/global split：接近 error locality 的定义性属性，但实证量化有增量贡献。[部分共识]\\n- 建议 frame 为\\\"经验验证\\\"而非\\\"新发现\\\"。\\n\\n**[SF-4] AgentDebug 重叠讨论不足** [R2-W2 | R1 同意]\\n- AgentDebug 在 ALFWorld 提出 5 维 error taxonomy 且发现 error cascading 是主要瓶颈，与 §3.5 C1 cascading 直接重叠。\\n- §6 仅一句话提及，需详细对比两套 taxonomy 和 cascading 发现。\\n\\n**[SF-5] 单 seed 消融可靠性不足** [R2-W3 | R1 同意]\\n- Dose-response（Table 2）和 per-operator（Figure 2）均仅 seed=42。\\n- 主实验 TaxEI 的 seed 间方差极大（s0=46.8% vs s42/s123=61.7%），单 seed 结论可靠性存疑。\\n\\n**[SF-6] Related Work 缺 ALFWorld training-time 方法对比** [R2-W4 | R1 同意]\\n- §6 \\\"ALFWorld methods\\\" 段列出的几乎全是 inference-time 方法，缺 training-time 对比。\\n\\n**[SF-7] Title/claim scope vs 实际实验** [R2-W5 | R1 部分反驳]\\n- \\\"Embodied Planning\\\" 但仅测 ALFWorld（text-based interface）。\\n- R1 指出 ALFWorld 原论文标题即含 \\\"Embodied\\\"，用词符合文献惯例。但 text-based 接口使 errors 相对 discrete（如 \\\"Nothing happens\\\"），反而削弱论文 \\\"errors 不够 discrete\\\" 的论证。\\n- 建议在 Limitations 中讨论 text-based vs vision-based 区别。\\n\\n**[SF-8] 缺少其他数据增强方法对比** [R1-W5 | R2 同意]\\n- 无法区分 \\\"error injection 没帮助\\\" 和 \\\"任何 off-distribution augmentation 在小数据 SFT 下都 harmful\\\"。\\n- 应在 Discussion 中承认此限制。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]\\n\\n以下问题双方完全一致：\\n1. Recovery-pairing confound 是论文最根本的 framing 问题\\n2. Variance gradient 不应作为 boundary condition（统计完全不支持）\\n3. 主体文本超页数限制，需压缩\\n4. PALADIN +57pp 数字需准确归因\\n5. Per-operator 分析应报告 exact task count\\n6. AgentDebug 需更深入讨论\\n7. Abstract \\\"no improvement\\\" → \\\"no statistically significant improvement\\\"\\n8. 0% error detection rate 值得更突出讨论\\n\\n## Reviewer Divergence [分歧]\\n\\n1. **+57pp 和页数超限的严重性**：R2 列 must-fix，R1 初始列 should-fix（交叉后 AGREE）。**裁定：must-fix**——workshop 页数限制是硬约束，+57pp 影响 framing 基础。\\n2. **Training-evaluation paradox 解释**：R1 偏 distribution shift，R2 偏 memorization（10% 非线性为证据）。**裁定**：两种解释可共存，论文应并列讨论。\\n3. **Dose-response threshold 新颖性**：R2 认为已知，R1 认为与 Rolnick 2017 的 vision 对比有新意。**裁定**：R1 论证更具说服力——不同 domain 的对比确有增量贡献，但应明确标记为经验验证。\\n4. **Title \\\"Embodied Planning\\\"**：R2 认为 scope 过宽，R1 认为符合文献惯例。**裁定**：Title 可保留，但需在 Limitations 讨论 text-based vs vision-based 区别。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Questions（需作者澄清）\\n\\n1. §5.2 估计 PALADIN retrieval 贡献约 30pp，此数字来源？论文未引用 PALADIN 消融实验。\\n2. 如果 error injection without recovery pairing 在 tool-use 上也不 work，boundary conditions 框架是否成立？\\n3. Dose-response 消融为何选择 seed=42 而非 3-seed？是资源限制还是设计选择？\\n4. 0% error detection rate 是否意味着 taxonomy labels 完全未被模型学到？这对 TaxEI 的机制假说有何影响？\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Missing References\\n\\n1. **AgentDebug** (Zhu et al., 2025) 需更深入讨论——5 维 error taxonomy + error cascading 发现直接相关\\n2. ALFWorld 上的 training-time 数据增强方法（如有）\\n3. Embodied AI 中的 adversarial training / error injection 工作\\n4. Negative results 方法论文献中的 methodological precedents\\n\\n---\\n\\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\\n\\n外部审稿未启用（项目配置中 `external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled` 未设置）。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Overall Assessment\\n\\n**评分: Weak Reject (偏向 Borderline)**\\n**置信度: High (4/5)**\\n\\n**核心判断**：论文选题有价值、实验透明诚实，适合 Negative Results Workshop。但存在一个根本性 framing 缺陷（recovery-pairing confound 使 \\\"迁移失败\\\" 归因不可靠）和多个统计功效问题（variance gradient 无支撑、单 seed 消融、per-operator N 过小）。三个 boundary conditions 中，仅 dose-response threshold 有一定新颖性和可靠性。\\n\\n**修改后可接收的条件**：\\n1. 修正 framing：限定为 \\\"unpaired error injection\\\" 的迁移测试，两个竞争假说并列\\n2. 降级 variance gradient 为方向性观察，从 boundary conditions 主列表移除\\n3. 降低所有 claim 强度，与证据水平匹配\\n4. 压缩至 ≤ 4 页\\n5. 准确归因 PALADIN +57pp\\n\\n修改难度中等，不涉及补充实验（虽然补充 seeds 会显著加强论文）。预计 1 轮修改后可达 borderline accept。\\n\\n**Reviewer 评分汇总**：\\n- Reviewer 1: Overall 3/5, Soundness 2.5/5, Excitement 3/5\\n- Reviewer 2: Overall 2.5/5, Soundness 2.5/5, Excitement 3/5\\n- **综合**: Weak Reject (2.75/5)\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Detailed Comments\\n\\n### §1 Introduction\\n- 第一句 +57pp 需拆分归因（MF-5）\\n- \\\"variance gradient\\\" 作为第一个 boundary condition 前置需降级（MF-2）\\n\\n### §2 Method\\n- Error taxonomy 设计合理，但 local/global 分类需更强理论依据\\n- \\\"Error-recovery pairs are not explicitly paired\\\"——这是与 PALADIN 的关键差异，应在此处（而非仅 §5.2）强调\\n\\n### §3 Experiments\\n- Table 1: 添加脚注明确 2.1× 数据量差异的论证逻辑（MF-3）\\n- Table 2: 标注 \\\"single seed, preliminary\\\"（SF-5）\\n- §3.1: \\\"no improvement\\\" → \\\"no statistically significant improvement\\\"\\n- §3.4: 0% detection rate 值得独立段落讨论\\n- §3.5: 报告 exact task count，如 \\\"C4: 7/11 (TaxEI) vs 6/11 (Vanilla)\\\"（SF-1）\\n- Figure 2: 增加 \\\"Exploratory, single seed\\\" 标注醒目度\\n\\n### §4 Discussion（原 §5）\\n- §5.1: 结构假说应与 recovery-pairing 假说并列\\n- §5.2: Recovery-pairing confound 应提升至 Introduction\\n- §5.3: 增加 text-based vs vision-based ALFWorld 的讨论（SF-7）\\n\\n### §6 Related Work\\n- AgentDebug: 扩展讨论，对比两套 taxonomy（SF-4）\\n- ALFWorld: 补充 training-time 方法（SF-6）\\n- 此 # Insights 2025 Workshop Submission Guidelines

## Page Limits
- **Short papers**: Up to 4 pages (excluding references, appendices, limitations, and broader impacts sections)
- **Non-archival abstracts**: 1-2 pages for papers published elsewhere
- **Appendices**: Unlimited pages but papers must be independently comprehensible

## Format Requirements
- Follow "ACL two-column format"
- Use official style sheets from the Overleaf template
- Adhere to "formatting guidelines outlined here" for paper size, margins, and fonts
- Do not modify style files or use templates from other conferences
- Non-compliant submissions face automatic rejection

## Submission Deadlines
- Direct submissions: January 30 / February 10, 2025
- ACL Rolling Review papers: February 20, 2025
- Acceptance notification: March 15, 2025
- Camera-ready due: March 15, 2025
- All deadlines: 11:59 pm UTC -12h

## Anonymity Rules
Workshop follows "the new ACL policy" on anonymity; previous work presentations should appear in footnotes only in camera-ready versions, not anonymized versions.

## Authorship
Author lists must include all contributors making substantial contributions; no changes permitted after submission deadline.

## Presentation Requirements
Accepted papers must be presented at the workshop for inclusion in proceedings. At least one author must register.

- ACL two-column format
- Double-blind anonymity (ACL policy)
- EMNLP 2026 is in Budapest, Hungary, October 22-29, 2026


- The 2026 workshop CFP hasn't been released yet, so the deadline is still unknown

**Paper state:**
- main.tex is complete with all sections
- Title: "When Does Unpaired Error Injection Help LLM Agents? Boundary Conditions from a Failed Transfer to Embodied Planning"
- Uses the correct ACL review template
- Properly anonymized

**Round 1 review issues (16 total, all in_progress):**
- 5 critical issues: data volume confound, lack of statistical support for variance claims, recovery-pairing confound, page limit violation, and PALADIN attribution
- 8 warning-level issues: per-operator granularity and training-eval concerns
- 3 info-level issues: statistical language, detection rate discussion, and \shortcite formatting

I'm now checking the remaining warning and info issues from Round 1 — the paradox and boundary conditions novelty, AgentDebug coverage, single-seed analyses, related work gaps, title scope alignment, and data augmentation comparisons, plus the statistical language and detection rate discussion points.

Looking at the submission message, it says "系统通知：审稿审批已通过。请开始审稿。" with "[论文提交]" — this doesn't explicitly signal either a continuation or fresh review. But since review_issues.yaml already exists with Round 1 issues, I need to determine whether this is a fresh submission or a continuation. The message format is closer to a fresh submission, but the existing Round 1 issues suggest this might be a follow-up. According to the guidelines, fresh mode treats this as an initial review without referencing previous content, so I should approach this as a new review cycle. Looking at the message more carefully, it just says "请开始审稿" without explicitly marking this as Round 2, but the existing review_issues.yaml from Round 1 with all issues marked "in_progress" suggests the paper has been revised and I'm checking whether those issues were addressed. I'll treat this as a continuation review, using the previous round's findings to track what's been fixed rather than starting completely fresh. I'm trying to figure out whether this is a continuation review or a fresh start. The kickoff message doesn't explicitly say "Round N - 继续审稿", just a system notification to begin review, but I have the previous round's review_issues.yaml which suggests this is Round 2 after the authors made revisions. Since the message format is ambiguous, I'll treat this as a fresh review while using the existing issues as a reference point to check what was actually addressed.

Now I need to decide on reviewer arrangement. The guidelines say workshop short papers get 1 reviewer for a full review, and while this paper had 16 issues in Round 1, a single comprehensive reviewer should be sufficient for a workshop submission. I'm settling on one reviewer for the comprehensive assessment since it's a workshop paper. Before I dispatch them, I need to check if the round_1.md report exists to see what issues were flagged previously, then I'll notify Group C and get the review underway.

2	round: 1

8	---
9	
10	# 审稿报告 — Round 1
11	
12	**论文**: "When Does Error Injection Help LLM Agents? Boundary Conditions from a Failed Transfer to Embodied Planning"
13	**目标 Venue**: Insights from Negative Results in NLP @ EMNLP 2026
14	**审稿模式**: 2 Reviewer 圆桌，1 轮独立审稿 + 交叉回应，1 轮收敛
15	**收敛状态**: 关键问题高度共识，无需 Round 2
16	
17	---
18	
19	## Summary
20	
21	本文测试 taxonomy-guided error injection（TaxEI）从 tool-use（PALADIN）向 embodied planning（ALFWorld）的迁移。在 Qwen3-8B 上对比 Vanilla/TaxEI/Random 三条件（3 seeds），发现 TaxEI 无改善（−8.6pp, p=0.054）。论文提出三个 boundary conditions：variance gradient、dose-response threshold、per-operator local/global split。论文定位为 negative result paper，目标 venue 为 EMNLP 2026 Negative Results Workshop。
22	
23	---
24	
25	## Strengths
26	
27	1. **选题精准，venue 匹配度高**：跨域迁移测试是自然的下一步，负面结果避免社区重复尝试。[R1, R2 共识]
28	2. **实验透明诚实**：Limitations 坦承几乎所有统计弱点，Version Discrepancy（Appendix D）透明披露，v3→v4 数据问题不隐瞒。[R1, R2 共识]
29	3. **超越简单报告失败**：dose-response、per-operator 分析和 structural comparison（Table 3）生成可检验假说。[R1]
30	4. **整体实验框架合理**：三条件 + 多 seed + dose-response 消融 + recovery eval 的设计逻辑完整。[R1]
31	5. **写作清晰流畅**：叙事有条理，实验细节充分（超参完整：LoRA rank 16, α=32, lr 1e-4, 3 epochs, bf16）。[R2]
32	
33	---
34	
35	## Weaknesses
36	
37	### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）
38	
39	**[MF-1] Recovery-pairing confound 使核心 "迁移失败" 结论不可靠** [R1-N3, R2-N2 | 双方最强共识]
40	- PALADIN = taxonomy tagging + recovery pairing + retrieval augmentation（~30pp 增益来自 retrieval）。TaxEI 仅复现了 taxonomy tagging。
41	- "迁移失败"无法区分：(a) error injection 在 embodied planning 本身无效 vs (b) 缺少 recovery pairing 导致失败。
42	- §5.2 承认了这点，但 Title/Abstract/Introduction/Conclusion 的 framing 暗示 (a)。
43	- **必须修改**：framing 明确限定为 "unpaired error injection" 的迁移测试；将 confound 从 Discussion 提升至 Introduction；两个竞争假说（task structure vs recovery pairing）并列为等权重解释。
44	
45	**[MF-2] Variance gradient 作为 boundary condition 无统计支撑** [R1-N2 | R2 强烈同意]
46	- 三组 SD=3.3/7.0/8.6pp，每组仅 n=3。所有检验均 NS（Levene's p=0.085, F-test p=0.252, bootstrap P=69%）。
47	- n=3 估 SD 的 95% CI 极宽（SD=3.3 → CI≈[1.9, 12.6]；SD=8.6 → CI≈[4.9, 33.0]），大幅重叠。
48	- 将统计上完全不支持的 pattern 列为论文第一个 boundary condition，违反 soundness 标准。
49	- **必须修改**：从 boundary conditions 列表中移除或降级为"方向性观察"，不在 Abstract 中以 claim 形式出现。
50	
51	**[MF-3] 数据量混淆与统计功效不足并存** [R1-N1 | R2 同意]
52	- 主表（Table 1）：TaxEI/Random 9,029 vs Vanilla 4,358（2.1×），有数据量混淆。
53	- 控制数据量的 dose-response 消融（Table 2）更公平，但仅用 seed=42（TaxEI 表现最好的 seed），可能系统性高估。
54	- 三个 boundary conditions 的证据基础全部建立在 underpowered 设计上。
55	- **必须修改**：(1) 将 Table 1 明确定位为"保守估计——TaxEI 即使多出 2.1× 数据仍更差"，把混淆转化为论证优势；(2) dose-response 补充 seeds 或明确标记为 preliminary；(3) 降低 claim 强度。
56	
57	**[MF-4] 主体文本超 4 页限制** [R2-N1, R1-W4 | 双方同意]
58	- PDF 确认 Conclusion 结束于 page 5 上半部，主体约 4.3–4.5 页。
59	- Insights workshop 短文 ≤ 4 pages excluding references，硬性格式违规。
60	- **必须修改**：大幅精简 Related Work（当前约 0.8 页），或将部分 Discussion 移至 Appendix。
61	
62	**[MF-5] PALADIN +57pp 归因不准确** [R2-N3, R1-W3 | 双方同意，R2 坚持 must-fix 级别]
63	- Abstract 第一句将 +57pp 归因于 error injection，但这是 PALADIN 完整系统增益（含 retrieval ~30pp）。
64	- 误导读者高估 error injection alone 的效果，扭曲论文出发点。
65	- **必须修改**：首次提及时拆分增益来源，区分 taxonomy injection 和 retrieval augmentation 的贡献。
66	
67	### Should-Fix
68	
69	**[SF-1] Per-operator local/global split 粒度过粗** [R1-W1 | R2 同意]
70	- N=11-12 per operator，单 seed，±1 task ≈ ±8-9pp。C4 的 +9.1pp 实际仅 +1 task。
71	- "Binary split" 措辞过强，可能是随机波动。
72	- 应报告 exact task count（如 "7/11 vs 6/11"），降级为 hypothesis-generating observation。
73	
74	**[SF-2] Training-evaluation paradox 缺替代假说** [R1-W2 | R2 部分反驳]
75	- 论文归因于 memorization，但 distribution shift 是更简单的竞争解释。
76	- R2 指出 10% 的非线性行为部分支持 memorization，但共识是应讨论两种解释。
77	- 在 §4.2 中增加 distribution shift 作为 competing explanation。
78	
79	**[SF-3] Boundary conditions 新颖性有限** [R2-W1 | R1 部分反驳]
80	- Variance gradient："加噪声增加不稳定性"可预期，无新颖性。[共识]
81	- Dose-response threshold：R1 指出与 Rolnick 2017（vision 对 massive noise robust）的对比有新意。[分歧]
82	- Local/global split：接近 error locality 的定义性属性，但实证量化有增量贡献。[部分共识]
83	- 建议 frame 为"经验验证"而非"新发现"。
84	
85	**[SF-4] AgentDebug 重叠讨论不足** [R2-W2 | R1 同意]
86	- AgentDebug 在 ALFWorld 提出 5 维 error taxonomy 且发现 error cascading 是主要瓶颈，与 §3.5 C1 cascading 直接重叠。
87	- §6 仅一句话提及，需详细对比两套 taxonomy 和 cascading 发现。
88	
89	**[SF-5] 单 seed 消融可靠性不足** [R2-W3 | R1 同意]
90	- Dose-response（Table 2）和 per-operator（Figure 2）均仅 seed=42。
91	- 主实验 TaxEI 的 seed 间方差极大（s0=46.8% vs s42/s123=61.7%），单 seed 结论可靠性存疑。
92	
93	**[SF-6] Related Work 缺 ALFWorld training-time 方法对比** [R2-W4 | R1 同意]
94	- §6 "ALFWorld methods" 段列出的几乎全是 inference-time 方法，缺 training-time 对比。
95	
96	**[SF-7] Title/claim scope vs 实际实验** [R2-W5 | R1 部分反驳]
97	- "Embodied Planning" 但仅测 ALFWorld（text-based interface）。
98	- R1 指出 ALFWorld 原论文标题即含 "Embodied"，用词符合文献惯例。但 text-based 接口使 errors 相对 discrete（如 "Nothing happens"），反而削弱论文 "errors 不够 discrete" 的论证。
99	- 建议在 Limitations 中讨论 text-based vs vision-based 区别。
100	
101	**[SF-8] 缺少其他数据增强方法对比** [R1-W5 | R2 同意]
102	- 无法区分 "error injection 没帮助" 和 "任何 off-distribution augmentation 在小数据 SFT 下都 harmful"。
103	- 应在 Discussion 中承认此限制。
104	
105	---
106	
107	## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]
108	
109	以下问题双方完全一致：
110	1. Recovery-pairing confound 是论文最根本的 framing 问题
111	2. Variance gradient 不应作为 boundary condition（统计完全不支持）
112	3. 主体文本超页数限制，需压缩
113	4. PALADIN +57pp 数字需准确归因
114	5. Per-operator 分析应报告 exact task count
115	6. AgentDebug 需更深入讨论
116	7. Abstract "no improvement" → "no statistically significant improvement"
117	8. 0% error detection rate 值得更突出讨论
118	
119	## Reviewer Divergence [分歧]
120	
121	1. **+57pp 和页数超限的严重性**：R2 列 must-fix，R1 初始列 should-fix（交叉后 AGREE）。**裁定：must-fix**——workshop 页数限制是硬约束，+57pp 影响 framing 基础。
122	2. **Training-evaluation paradox 解释**：R1 偏 distribution shift，R2 偏 memorization（10% 非线性为证据）。**裁定**：两种解释可共存，论文应并列讨论。
123	3. **Dose-response threshold 新颖性**：R2 认为已知，R1 认为与 Rolnick 2017 的 vision 对比有新意。**裁定**：R1 论证更具说服力——不同 domain 的对比确有增量贡献，但应明确标记为经验验证。
124	4. **Title "Embodied Planning"**：R2 认为 scope 过宽，R1 认为符合文献惯例。**裁定**：Title 可保留，但需在 Limitations 讨论 text-based vs vision-based 区别。
125	
126	---
127	
128	## Questions（需作者澄清）
129	
130	1. §5.2 估计 PALADIN retrieval 贡献约 30pp，此数字来源？论文未引用 PALADIN 消融实验。
131	2. 如果 error injection without recovery pairing 在 tool-use 上也不 work，boundary conditions 框架是否成立？
132	3. Dose-response 消融为何选择 seed=42 而非 3-seed？是资源限制还是设计选择？
133	4. 0% error detection rate 是否意味着 taxonomy labels 完全未被模型学到？这对 TaxEI 的机制假说有何影响？
134	
135	---
136	
137	## Missing References
138	
139	1. **AgentDebug** (Zhu et al., 2025) 需更深入讨论——5 维 error taxonomy + error cascading 发现直接相关
140	2. ALFWorld 上的 training-time 数据增强方法（如有）
141	3. Embodied AI 中的 adversarial training / error injection 工作
142	4. Negative results 方法论文献中的 methodological precedents
143	
144	---
145	
146	## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)
147	
148	外部审稿未启用（项目配置中 `external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled` 未设置）。
149	
150	---
151	
152	## Overall Assessment
153	
154	**评分: Weak Reject (偏向 Borderline)**
155	**置信度: High (4/5)**
156	
157	**核心判断**：论文选题有价值、实验透明诚实，适合 Negative Results Workshop。但存在一个根本性 framing 缺陷（recovery-pairing confound 使 "迁移失败" 归因不可靠）和多个统计功效问题（variance gradient 无支撑、单 seed 消融、per-operator N 过小）。三个 boundary conditions 中，仅 dose-response threshold 有一定新颖性和可靠性。
158	
159	**修改后可接收的条件**：
160	1. 修正 framing：限定为 "unpaired error injection" 的迁移测试，两个竞争假说并列
161	2. 降级 variance gradient 为方向性观察，从 boundary conditions 主列表移除
162	3. 降低所有 claim 强度，与证据水平匹配
163	4. 压缩至 ≤ 4 页
164	5. 准确归因 PALADIN +57pp
165	
166	修改难度中等，不涉及补充实验（虽然补充 seeds 会显著加强论文）。预计 1 轮修改后可达 borderline accept。
167	
168	**Reviewer 评分汇总**：
169	- Reviewer 1: Overall 3/5, Soundness 2.5/5, Excitement 3/5
170	- Reviewer 2: Overall 2.5/5, Soundness 2.5/5, Excitement 3/5
171	- **综合**: Weak Reject (2.75/5)
172	
173	---
174	
175	## Detailed Comments
176	
177	### §1 Introduction
178	- 第一句 +57pp 需拆分归因（MF-5）
179	- "variance gradient" 作为第一个 boundary condition 前置需降级（MF-2）
180	
181	### §2 Method
182	- Error taxonomy 设计合理，但 local/global 分类需更强理论依据
183	- "Error-recovery pairs are not explicitly paired"——这是与 PALADIN 的关键差异，应在此处（而非仅 §5.2）强调
184	
185	### §3 Experiments
186	- Table 1: 添加脚注明确 2.1× 数据量差异的论证逻辑（MF-3）
187	- Table 2: 标注 "single seed, preliminary"（SF-5）
188	- §3.1: "no improvement" → "no statistically significant improvement"
189	- §3.4: 0% detection rate 值得独立段落讨论
190	- §3.5: 报告 exact task count，如 "C4: 7/11 (TaxEI) vs 6/11 (Vanilla)"（SF-1）
191	- Figure 2: 增加 "Exploratory, single seed" 标注醒目度
192	
193	### §4 Discussion（原 §5）
194	- §5.1: 结构假说应与 recovery-pairing 假说并列
195	- §5.2: Recovery-pairing confound 应提升至 Introduction
196	- §5.3: 增加 text-based vs vision-based ALFWorld 的讨论（SF-7）
197	
198	### §6 Related Work
199	- AgentDebug: 扩展讨论，对比两套 taxonomy（SF-4）
200	- ALFWorld: 补充 training-time 方法（SF-6）
201	- 此 section 是压缩页数的主要候选
202	
203	### Appendix
204	- Table 3 (structural comparison) 有用，可保留
205	- Training dynamics 和 per-task analysis 支撑主文 claims
```

Introduction；两个竞争假说（task structure vs recovery pairing）并列为等权重解释。\n\n**[MF-2] Variance gradient 作为 boundary condition 无统计支撑** [R1-N2 | R2 强烈同意]\n- 三组 SD=3.3/7.0/8.6pp，每组仅 n=3。所有检验均 NS（Levene's p=0.085, F-test p=0.252, bootstrap P=69%）。\n- n=3 估 SD 的 95% CI 极宽（SD=3.3 → CI≈[1.9, 12.6]；SD=8.6 → CI≈[4.9, 33.0]），大幅重叠。\n- 将统计上完全不支持的 pattern 列为论文第一个 boundary condition，违反 soundness 标准。\n- **必须修改**：从 boundary conditions 列表中移除或降级为\"方向性观察\"，不在 Abstract 中以 claim 形式出现。\n\n**[MF-3] 数据量混淆与统计功效不足并存** [R1-N1 | R2 同意]\n- 主表（Table 1）：TaxEI/Random 9,029 vs Vanilla 4,358（2.1×），有数据量混淆。\n- 控制数据量的 dose-response 消融（Table 2）更公平，但仅用 seed=42（TaxEI 表现最好的 seed），可能系统性高估。\n- 三个 boundary conditions 的证据基础全部建立在 underpowered 设计上。\n- **必须修改**：(1) 将 Table 1 明确定位为\"保守估计——TaxEI 即使多出 2.1× 数据仍更差\"，把混淆转化为论证优势；(2) dose-response 补充 seeds 或明确标记为 preliminary；(3) 降低 claim 强度。\n\n**[MF-4] 主体文本超 4 页限制** [R2-N1, R1-W4 | 双方同意]\n- PDF 确认 Conclusion 结束于 page 5 上半部，主体约 4.3–4.5 页。\n- Insights workshop 短文 ≤ 4 pages excluding references，硬性格式违规。\n- **必须修改**：大幅精简 Related Work（当前约 0.8 页），或将部分 Discussion 移至 Appendix。\n\n**[MF-5] PALADIN +57pp 归因不准确** [R2-N3, R1-W3 | 双方同意，R2 坚持 must-fix 级别]\n- Abstract 第一句将 +57pp 归因于 error injection，但这是 PALADIN 完整系统增益（含 retrieval ~30pp）。\n- 误导读者高估 error injection alone 的效果，扭曲论文出发点。\n- **必须修改**：首次提及时拆分增益来源，区分 taxonomy injection 和 retrieval augmentation 的贡献。\n\n### Should-Fix\n\n**[SF-1] Per-operator local/global split 粒度过粗** [R1-W1 | R2 同意]\n- N=11-12 per operator，单 seed，±1 task ≈ ±8-9pp。C4 的 +9.1pp 实际仅 +1 task。\n- \"Binary split\" 措辞过强，可能是随机波动。\n- 应报告 exact task count（如 \"7/11 vs 6/11\"），降级为 hypothesis-generating observation。\n\n**[SF-2] Training-evaluation paradox 缺替代假说** [R1-W2 | R2 部分反驳]\n- 论文归因于 memorization，但 distribution shift 是更简单的竞争解释。\n- R2 指出 10% 的非线性行为部分支持 memorization，但共识是应讨论两种解释。\n- 在 §4.2 中增加 distribution shift 作为 competing explanation。\n\n**[SF-3] Boundary conditions 新颖性有限** [R2-W1 | R1 部分反驳]\n- Variance gradient：\"加噪声增加不稳定性\"可预期，无新颖性。[共识]\n- Dose-response threshold：R1 指出与 Rolnick 2017（vision 对 massive noise robust）的对比有新意。[分歧]\n- Local/global split：接近 error locality 的定义性属性，但实证量化有增量贡献。[部分共识]\n- 建议 frame 为\"经验验证\"而非\"新发现\"。\n\n**[SF-4] 消融可靠性不足** [R2-W3 | R1 同意]\n- Dose-response（Table 2）和 per-operator（Figure 2）均仅 seed=42。\n- 主实验 TaxEI 的 seed 间方差极大（s0=46.8% vs s42/s123=61.7%），单 seed 结论可靠性存疑。\n\n**[SF-6] Related Work 缺 ALFWorld training-time 方法对比** [R2-W4 | R1 同意]\n- §6 \"ALFWorld methods\" 段列出的几乎全是 inference-time 方法，缺 training-time 对比。\n\n**[SF-7] Title/claim scope vs 实际实验** [R2-W5 | R1 部分反驳]\n- \"Embodied Planning\" 但仅测 ALFWorld（text-based interface）。\n- R1 指出 ALFWorld 原论文标题即含 \"Embodied\"，用词符合文献惯例。但 text-based 接口使 errors 相对 discrete（如 \"Nothing happens\"），反而削弱论文 \"errors 不够 discrete\" 的论证。\n- 建议在 Limitations 中讨论 text-based vs vision-based 区别。\n\n**[SF-8] 缺少其他数据增强方法对比** [R1-W5 | R2 同意]\n- 无法区分 \"error injection 没帮助\" 和 \"任何 off-distribution augmentation 在小数据 SFT 下都 harmful\"。\n- 应在 Discussion 中承认此限制。\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus [AGREE]\n\n以下问题双方完全一致：\n1. AgentDebug 需更深入讨论\n7. Abstract \"no improvement\" → \"no statistically significant improvement\"\n8. 0% error detection rate 值得更突出讨论\n\n## Reviewer Divergence [分歧]\n\n1. **+57pp 和页数超限的严重性**：R2 列 must-fix，R1 初始列 should-fix（交叉后 AGREE）。**裁定：must-fix**——workshop 页数限制是硬约束，+57pp 影响 framing 基础。\n2. **Training-evaluation paradox 解释**：R1 偏 distribution shift，R2 偏 memorization（10% 非线性为证据）。**裁定**：两种解释可共存，论文应并列讨论。\n3. **Dose-response threshold 新颖性**：R2 认为已知，R1 认为与 Rolnick 2017 的 vision 对比有新意。**裁定**：R1 论证更具说服力——不同 domain 的对比确有增量贡献，但应明确标记为经验验证。\n4. **Title \"Embodied Planning\"**：R2 认为 scope 过宽，R1 认为符合文献惯例。**裁定**：Title 可保留，但需在 Limitations 讨论 text-based vs vision-based 区别。\n\n---\n\n## Questions（需作者澄清）\n\n1. §5.2 估计 PALADIN retrieval 贡献约 30pp，此数字来源？论文未引用 PALADIN 消融实验。\n2. 如果 error injection without recovery pairing 在 tool-use 上也不 work，boundary conditions 框架是否成立？\n3. Dose-response 消融为何选择 seed=42 而非 3-seed？是资源限制还是设计选择？\n4. 0% error detection rate 是否意味着 taxonomy labels 完全未被模型学到？这对 TaxEI 的机制假说有何影响？\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n1. **AgentDebug** (Zhu et al., 2025) 需更深入讨论——5 维 error taxonomy + error cascading 发现直接相关\n2. Negative results 方法论文献中的 methodological precedents\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n外部审稿未启用（项目配置中 `external_review.paperreview_ai.enabled` 未设置）。\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**评分: Weak Reject (偏向 Borderline)**\n**置信度: High (4/5)**\n\n**核心判断**：论文选题有价值、实验透明诚实，适合 Negative Results Workshop。但存在一个根本性 framing 缺陷（recovery-pairing confound 使 \"迁移失败\"    - Intro §1 paragraph 2 addresses the confound ✓
   - Intro lists "Negative transfer with competing hypotheses" as contribution 1 ✓
   - Discussion and Conclusion both reference the two competing hypotheses ✓

The contributions have been reframed to focus on negative transfer with competing hypotheses and the dose-dependent dual regime.

3. **MF-3 (Data volume confound)**: Table 1 now properly controls for data volume differences, and the dose-response ablation includes multi-seed runs at different percentages, which is a meaningful improvement.

4. **MF-4 (Page limit violation)**: Checking the current page count across sections to see if we're within the ACL format constraints.  The analysis section has been folded into experiments to save space, and related work is now more compact, though I'd need to compile to get an exact count.

5. **MF-5 (PALADIN +57pp attribution)**: The abstract and intro now explicitly call out all three components of PALADIN—taxonomy-guided injection, recovery pairing, and inference-time retrieval—making the attribution clearer.

6. **SF-1 (Per-operator exact task counts)**: The paper reports specific task counts for each operator (like C4 observation masking: 7/11 vs. 6/11, +9.1pp) and Figure 2 notes the sample sizes and uncertainty bounds.

7. **SF-2 (Training-eval paradox alternative hypotheses)**: Looking at how the paper addresses the discrepancy between training and evaluation performance with plausible explanations.

8. **SF-4 (AgentDebug)**: The related work section provides a detailed comparison with AgentDebug, noting that while both identify error cascading, their approaches differ—AgentDebug classifies errors post-hoc whereas this work uses controlled perturbations to generate them.

9. **SF-5 (Single seed dose-response)**: Table 2 now includes 3-seed results for the 25% and 50% injection levels, with only the 10% condition remaining single-seeded.

10. **SF-6 (ALFWorld training-time methods)**: The related work mentions CoEx and AgentPRM as existing ALFWorld training methods, then explicitly states that no prior work has tested training-time error injection on this benchmark.

11. **SF-7 (Title scope - embodied planning)**: The limitations section acknowledges that ALFWorld's text-based interface with discrete error feedback may not generalize to vision-based embodied tasks with continuous state spaces.

12. **SF-8 (Other augmentation methods)**: Only error injection variants were compared; other augmentation strategies like paraphrasing or trajectory diversification weren't explored.

13. **R1-M1 (Statistical language)**: Both the abstract and results section properly report that TaxEI shows no statistically significant improvement, with p-values and permutation test details included.

14. **R1-M2 (0% detection rate)**: The uniform 0% detection rate across all models suggests the taxonomy labels weren't actually learned during supervised fine-tuning.

15. **RR-1 (v3→v4 discrepancy)**: Appendix D documents the version transition in detail.

16. **SF-3 (Boundary conditions novelty)**: The contributions have been reframed around negative transfer with competing hypotheses and dose-dependent dual regimes rather than three separate boundary conditions, with the per-operator analysis positioned as exploratory.

Now checking the \shortcite formatting...

The paper's addressed most Round 1 feedback—the recovery-pairing confound is properly contextualized, PALADIN attribution corrected, multi-seed dose-response data included at 25% and 50%, and statistical language tightened. Two alternative explanations for the training-eval paradox are now presented. The main remaining question is whether the page limit holds after merging analysis into experiments and condensing related work, and I need to verify the dose-response baseline reporting matches across the table and documentation. I see the issue now—the 10% single-seed row is being compared to the 3-seed vanilla mean (65.3%) when it should be compared to the vanilla seed=42 baseline (61.7%), which would give Δ=0pp instead of -3.6pp. The 25% and 50% rows with 3-seed data are correctly compared to the 3-seed mean, but this inconsistency in the 10% row needs to be fixed in the table. Looking at the text descriptions, the abstract correctly reports the 3-seed comparison (-8.6pp), and the introduction properly frames the 25% degradation against the vanilla mean of 65.3. The main issue is that the table's Δ column conflates different baselines at 10%, making it inconsistent with how the text describes those results—the text acknowledges TaxEI matches vanilla at 10% (seed=42), but the table suggests otherwise.

The "Boundary Conditions from a Failed Transfer" title is somewhat misleading since the paper doesn't explicitly enumerate boundary conditions as named items, though the conclusion's four properties (discrete errors, local recovery, immediate feedback, recovery-pairing confound) serve a similar purpose and are framed appropriately as requiring validation elsewhere.

One concern is that the abstract is quite dense for a 4-page workshop paper, packing in dose-response numbers, standard deviations, and per-operator breakdowns that might be better reserved for the main text.

There was a floating-point precision bug in the permutation test that Memory caught—the corrected p-value for TaxEI versus Vanilla is 0.054 rather than 0.017, and the paper correctly reports the fixed value. The new dose-response data across three seeds shows interesting patterns: at 25% TaxEI the results converge tightly around 47.5±1.2, but at 50% there's substantial variance (56.0±12.4) driven by one seed performing much better than the others.

Looking at the Random baseline results, the 25% condition shows moderate spread (56.0±5.3) while 50% is more variable (58.1±11.7), which reinforces that the tight convergence at 25% TaxEI is genuinely remarkable and supports the dual-regime hypothesis the authors are proposing.

I've reviewed the major issues from Round 1 and they've been substantially addressed, so I'm ready to move forward with the review. Given the significant improvements and the need for thoroughness, I'll assign two reviewers rather than one.

Now I'm setting up the reviewer assignments with specific focus areas: one reviewer will handle methodology, experimental design, and statistical rigor, while the other covers framing, writing quality, and format compliance.
